- **clean-code-principles**: Clean Code principles from Robert C. Martin — naming, function design, comments, error handling, and class cohesion. - **clean-code-review**: Perform a read-only, evidence-based code quality review focused on correctness, clarity, maintainability, tests, security, and framework conventions. Use when the user asks to review code, audit code quality, find code smells, assess maintainability, or asks whether code is ready for another developer. Do not edit files; use boy-scout-cleanup for small safe edits or /refactor for structural changes. ([tmoody1973/clean-code-claude-code](https://github.com/tmoody1973/clean-code-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/clean-code-review))
